These variations differ on how virtues relate to human flourishing, agent motivations, and specific contexts while maintaining that virtue is central to ethical life."

          "snippet": "The concept of liberty is fundamentally analyzed through the distinction between negative and positive senses, a framework established by Isaiah Berlin but subject to various interpretations regarding freedom as non-domination or triadic relations. This ongoing discourse highlights how definitions shift based on philosophical priorities such as autonomy, coercion, and structural oppression."
